count.\n245\tfunc (s *State) DecrementActiveMessageCount() {\n246\t\ts.mu.Lock()\n247\t\tdefer s.mu.Unlock()\n248\t\tif s.activeMessageCount > 0 {\n249\t\t\ts.activeMessageCount--\n250\t\t}\n251\t\ts.lastUpdate = time.Now()\n252\t}\n253\t\n254\t// tickMsg is sent periodically to update the UI.\n255\ttype tickMsg time.Time\n256\t\n257\t// eventReceivedMsg is sent when an event is received from the socket.\n258\ttype eventReceivedMsg Event\n259\t\n260\t// connectedMsg is sent when successfully connected to the event socket.\n261\ttype connectedMsg struct {\n262\t\tConn net.Conn\n263\t}\n264\t\n265\t// disconnectedMsg is sent when disconnected from the event socket.\n266\ttype disconnectedMsg struct {\n267\t\terr error\n268\t}\n269\t\n270\t// eventWithConnMsg carries an event along with its connection.\n271\ttype eventWithConnMsg struct {\n272\t\tEvent Event\n273\t\tConn  net.Conn\n274\t}\n275\t\n276\t// model is the Bubble Tea model for the dashboard.\n277\ttype model struct {\n278\t\tstate          *State\n279\t\tconn           net.Conn\n280\t\treconnectDelay time.Duration\n281\t\twidth          int\n282\t\theight         int\n283\t\tconnected      bool\n284\t\tlastError      error\n285\t\tquitting       bool\n286\t}\n287\t\n288\t// initialModel creates the initial model.\n289\tfunc initialModel() model {\n290\t\treturn model{\n291\t\t\tstate:          NewState(),\n292\t\t\treconnectDelay: time.Second,\n293\t\t\twidth:          80,\n294\t\t\theight:         24,\n295\t\t\tconnected:      false,\n296\t\t}\n297\t}\n298\t\n299\t// Init initializes the model.\n300\tfunc (m model) Init() tea.Cmd {\n301\t\treturn tea.Batch(\n302\t\t\tconnectCmd(),\n303\t\t\ttickCmd(),\n304\t\t)\n305\t}\n306\t\n307\t// Update handles messages and updates the model.\n308\tfunc (m model) Update(msg tea.Msg) (tea.Model, tea.Cmd) {\n309\t\tswitch msg := msg.(type) {\n310\t\tcase tea.KeyMsg:\n311\t\t\tswitch msg.String() {\n312\t\t\tcase \"q\", \"ctrl+c\":\n313\t\t\t\tm.quitting = true\n314\t\t\t\treturn m, tea.Quit\n315\t\t\tcase \"r\":\n316\t\t\t\t// Force reconnect\n317\t\t\t\tif m.conn != nil {\n318\t\t\t\t\tm.conn.Close()\n319\t\t\t\t\tm.conn = nil\n320\t\t\t\t}\n321\t\t\t\tm.connected = false\n322\t\t\t\treturn m, connectCmd()\n323\t\t\t}\n324\t\tcase connectedMsg:\n325\t\t\tm.connected = true\n326\t\t\tm.lastError = nil\n327\t\t\tm.reconnectDelay = time.Second\n328\t\t\tif m.conn != nil {\n329\t\t\t\tm.conn.Close()\n330\t\t\t}\n331\t\t\tm.conn = msg.Conn\n332\t\t\treturn m, waitForNextEvent(msg.Conn)\n333\t\tcase disconnectedMsg:\n334\t\t\tm.connected = false\n335\t\t\tm.lastError = msg.err\n336\t\t\tif m.conn != nil {\n337\t\t\t\tm.conn.Close()\n338\t\t\t\tm.conn = nil\n339\t\t\t}\n340\t\t\treturn m, tea.Tick(m.reconnectDelay, func(t time.Time) tea.Msg {\n341\t\t\t\treturn connectedMsg{}\n342\t\t\t})\n343\t\tcase eventWithConnMsg:\n344\t\t\t// Store the connection and handle the event\n345\t\t\tif m.conn != nil {\n346\t\t\t\tm.conn.Close()\n347\t\t\t}\n348\t\t\tm.conn = msg.Conn\n349\t\t\tm.connected = true\n350\t\t\tm.lastError = nil\n351\t\t\tm.handleEvent(msg.Event)\n352\t\t\treturn m, waitForNextEvent(msg.Conn)\n353\t\tcase eventReceivedMsg:\n354\t\t\tm.handleEvent(Event(msg))\n355\t\t\treturn m, readEventsCmd()\n356\t\tcase tickMsg:\n357\t\t\treturn m, tickCmd()\n358\t\tcase tea.WindowSizeMsg:\n359\t\t\tm.width = msg.Width\n360\t\t\tm.height = msg.Height\n361\t\t\treturn m, nil\n362\t\t}\n363\t\treturn m, nil\n364\t}\n365\t\n366\t// handleEvent processes a single event from the stream.\n367\tfunc (m *model) handleEvent(event Event) {\n368\t\tswitch event.Type {\n369\t\tcase \"session_created\", \"session_updated\":\n370\t\t\tchatID := int64(event.Data[\"chat_id\"].(float64))\n371\t\t\tthreadID := int64(event.Data[\"thread_id\"].(float64))\n372\t\t\tsessionID := event.Data[\"session_id\"].(string)\n373\t\t\tmodel := \"\"\n374\t\t\tif m, ok := event.Data[\"model\"].(string); ok {\n375\t\t\t\tmodel = m\n376\t\t\t}\n377\t\t\tstatus := \"\"\n378\t\t\tif s, ok := event.Data[\"status\"].(string); ok {\n379\t\t\t\tstatus = s\n380\t\t\t}\n381\t\t\tnotifMode := \"live\"\n382\t\t\tif nm, ok := event.Data[\"notification_mode\"].(string); ok {\n383\t\t\t\tnotifMode = nm\n384\t\t\t}\n385\t\t\tcwd := \"\"\n386\t\t\tif c, ok := event.Data[\"cwd\"].(string); ok {\n387\t\t\t\tcwd = c\n388\t\t\t}\n389\t\t\tmsgCount := 0\n390\t\t\tif mc, ok := event.Data[\"message_count\"].(float64); ok {\n391\t\t\t\tmsgCount = int(mc)\n392\t\t\t}\n393\t\t\tcost := 0.0\n394\t\t\tif c, ok := event.Data[\"total_cost_usd\"].(float64); ok {\n395\t\t\t\tcost = c\n396\t\t\t}\n397\t\t\tm.state.UpdateSession(chatID, threadID, sessionID, model, status, notifMode, cwd, msgCount, cost)\n398\t\tcase \"session_closed\":\n399\t\t\tchatID := int64(event.Data[\"chat_id\"].(float64))\n400\t\t\tthreadID := int64(event.Data[\"thread_id\"].(float64))\n401\t\t\tm.state.RemoveSession(chatID, threadID)\n402\t\tcase \"message_received\":\n403\t\t\tchatID := int64(event.Data[\"chat_id\"].(float64))\n404\t\t\tthreadID := int64(event.Data[\"thread_id\"].(float64))\n405\t\t\tmessageID := int64(event.Data[\"message_id\"].(float64))\n406\t\t\tcontentType := \"\"\n407\t\t\tif ct, ok := event.Data[\"content_type\"].(string); ok {\n408\t\t\t\tcontentType = ct\n409\t\t\t}\n410\t\t\tuserID := int64(event.Data[\"user_id\"].(float64))\n411\t\t\tm.state.AddLogEntry(fmt.Sprintf(\"MSG IN ch=%d th=%d msg=%d type=%s uid=%d\", chatID, threadID, messageID, contentType, userID))\n412\t\t\tm.state.IncrementActiveMessageCount()\n413\t\tcase \"message_sent\":\n414\t\t\tchatID := int64(event.Data[\"chat_id\"].(float64))\n415\t\t\tthreadID := int64(event.Data[\"thread_id\"].(float64))\n416\t\t\tmessageID := int64(event.Data[\"message_id\"].(float64))\n417\t\t\tpurpose := \"\"\n418\t\t\tif p, ok := event.Data[\"purpose\"].(string); ok {\n419\t\t\t\tpurpose = p\n420\t\t\t}\n421\t\t\tm.state.AddLogEntry(fmt.Sprintf(\"MSG OUT ch=%d th=%d msg=%d purp=%s\", chatID, threadID, messageID, purpose))\n422\t\t\tm.state.DecrementActiveMessageCount()\n423\t\tcase \"command_executed\":\n424\t\t\tchatID := int64(event.Data[\"chat_id\"].(float64))\n425\t\t\tcommand := \"\"\n426\t\t\tif c, ok := event.Data[\"command\"].(string); ok {\n427\t\t\t\tcommand = c\n428\t\t\t}\n429\t\t\tuserID := int64(event.Data[\"user_id\"].(float64))\n430\t\t\tsuccess := false\n431\t\t\tif s, ok := event.Data[\"success\"].(bool); ok {\n432\t\t\t\tsuccess = s\n433\t\t\t}\n434\t\t\tm.state.AddCommandEntry(command, userID, success)\n435\t\t\tm.state.AddLogEntry(fmt.Sprintf(\"CMD ch=%d %s uid=%d ok=%t\", chatID, command, userID, success))\n436\t\tcase \"cost_recorded\":\n437\t\t\tchatID := int64(event.Data[\"chat_id\"].(float64))\n438\t\t\tthreadID := int64(event.Data[\"thread_id\"].(float64))\n439\t\t\tcostUSD := 0.0\n440\t\t\tif c, ok := event.Data[\"cost_usd\"].(float64); ok {\n441\t\t\t\tcostUSD = c\n442\t\t\t}\n443\t\t\tmodel := \"\"\n444\t\t\tif m, ok := event.Data[\"model\"].(string); ok {\n445\t\t\t\tmodel = m\n446\t\t\t}\n447\t\t\tm.state.AddLogEntry(fmt.Sprintf(\"COST ch=%d th=%d $%.4f %s\", chatID, threadID, costUSD, model))\n448\t\tcase \"health_check\":\n449\t\t\thealthy := false\n450\t\t\tif h, ok := event.Data[\"healthy\"].(bool); ok {\n451\t\t\t\thealthy = h\n452\t\t\t}\n453\t\t\tchecks := []HealthCheck{}\n454\t\t\tif c, ok := event.Data[\"checks\"].([]interface{}); ok {\n455\t\t\t\tfor _, checkItem := range c {\n456\t\t\t\t\tif checkMap, ok := checkItem.(map[string]interface{}); ok {\n457\t\t\t\t\t\tcheck := HealthCheck{}\n458\t\t\t\t\t\tif name, ok := checkMap[\"name\"].(string); ok {\n459\t\t\t\t\t\t\tcheck.Name = name\n460\t\t\t\t\t\t}\n461\t\t\t\t\t\tif healthy, ok := checkMap[\"healthy\"].(bool); ok {\n462\t\t\t\t\t\t\tcheck.Healthy = healthy\n463\t\t\t\t\t\t}\n464\t\t\t\t\t\tif msg, ok := checkMap[\"message\"].(string); ok {\n465\t\t\t\t\t\t\tcheck.Message = msg\n466\t\t\t\t\t\t}\n467\t\t\t\t\t\tchecks = append(checks, check)\n468\t\t\t\t\t}\n469\t\t\t\t}\n470\t\t\t}\n471\t\t\tm.state.UpdateHealth(healthy, checks)\n472\t\tcase \"system_error\":\n473\t\t\tcomponent := \"\"\n474\t\t\tif c, ok := event.Data[\"component\"].(string); ok {\n475\t\t\t\tcomponent = c\n476\t\t\t}\n477\t\t\tmessage := \"\"\n478\t\t\tif m, ok := event.Data[\"message\"].(string); ok {\n479\t\t\t\tmessage = m\n480\t\t\t}\n481\t\t\tm.state.AddLogEntry(fmt.Sprintf(\"ERROR [%s] %s\", component, message))\n482\t\tcase \"system_info\":\n483\t\t\tmessage := \"\"\n484\t\t\tif m, ok := event.Data[\"message\"].(string); ok {\n485\t\t\t\tmessage = m\n486\t\t\t}\n487\t\t\tm.state.AddLogEntry(fmt.Sprintf(\"INFO %s\", message))\n488\t\t}\n489\t}\n490\t\n491\t// View renders the UI.\n492\tfunc (m model) View() string {\n493\t\tif m.quitting {\n494\t\t\treturn \"Goodbye!\\n\"\n495\t\t}\n496\t\n497\t\t// Define styles\n498\t\ttitleStyle := lipgloss.NewStyle().\n499\t\t\tBold(true).\n500\t\t\tForeground(lipgloss.Color(\"86\")). // Light blue\n501\t\t\tMarginBottom(1)\n502\t\n503\t\tpanelStyle := lipgloss.NewStyle().\n504\t\t\tBorder(lipgloss.RoundedBorder()).\n505\t\t\tBorderForeground(lipgloss.Color(\"238\")). // Grey\n506\t\t\tPadding(0, 1)\n507\t\n508\t\theaderStyle := lipgloss.NewStyle().\n509\t\t\tBold(true).\n510\t\t\tForeground(lipgloss.Color(\"228\")). // Yellow\n511\t\t\tMarginBottom(1)\n512\t\n513\t\tsuccessStyle := lipgloss.NewStyle().Foreground(lipgloss.Color(\"142\")) // Green\n514\t\terrorStyle := lipgloss.NewStyle().Foreground(lipgloss.Color(\"203\"))   // Red\n515\t\twarningStyle := lipgloss.NewStyle().Foreground(lipgloss.Color(\"229\")) // Yellow\n516\t\tdimStyle := lipgloss.NewStyle().Foreground(lipgloss.Color(\"241\"))    // Grey\n517\t\tstatusStyle := lipgloss.NewStyle().Bold(true)                       // Bold for status\n518\t\n519\t\t// Calculate layout\n520\t\tleftWidth := m.width / 2\n521\t\trightWidth := m.width - leftWidth - 4 // 4 for borders/gaps\n522\t\n523\t\tsessionHeight := m.height / 2\n524\t\thealthHeight := 6\n525\t\tlogHeight := m.height - sessionHeight - healthHeight - 4 // 4 for headers/gaps\n526\t\n527\t\t// Header\n528\t\theader := titleStyle.Render(\"Telegram Claude Bridge Dashboard\")\n529\t\tif m.connected {\n530\t\t\theader += successStyle.Render(\" ● Connected\")\n531\t\t} else {\n532\t\t\theader += errorStyle.Render(\" ● Disconnected\")\n533\t\t\tif m.lastError != nil {\n534\t\t\t\theader += dimStyle.Render(fmt.Sprintf(\" (%s)\", m.lastError))\n535\t\t\t}\n536\t\t}\n537\t\theader += dimStyle.Render(\" | Press 'q' to quit, 'r' to reconnect\")\n538\t\n539\t\t// Active Sessions Panel\n540\t\tsessions := m.state.GetSessions()\n541\t\tsessionsPanel := headerStyle.Render(\"Active Sessions\") + \"\\n\"\n542\t\tif len(sessions) == 0 {\n543\t\t\tsessionsPanel += dimStyle.Render(\"No active sessions\")\n544\t\t} else {\n545\t\t\tfor i, sess := range sessions {\n546\t\t\t\tif i >= sessionHeight-2 {\n547\t\t\t\t\tbreak\n548\t\t\t\t}\n549\t\t\t\tstatusColor := successStyle\n550\t\t\t\tif sess.Status == \"blocked\" {\n551\t\t\t\t\tstatusColor = warningStyle\n552\t\t\t\t} else if sess.Status == \"error\" {\n553\t\t\t\t\tstatusColor = errorStyle\n554\t\t\t\t}\n555\t\t\t\tline := fmt.Sprintf(\"%s ch=%d th=%d %s msgs=%d cost=$%.4f\",\n556\t\t\t\t\tstatusStyle.Render(sess.Status),\n557\t\t\t\t\tsess.ChatID, sess.ThreadID,\n558\t\t\t\t\ttruncateString(sess.Model, 12),\n559\t\t\t\t\tsess.MessageCount, sess.TotalCostUSD,\n560\t\t\t\t)\n561\t\t\t\tif sess.NotificationMode != \"live\" {\n562\t\t\t\t\tline += dimStyle.Render(fmt.Sprintf(\" [%s]\", sess.NotificationMode))\n563\t\t\t\t}\n564\t\t\t\tsessionsPanel += statusColor.Render(line) + \"\\n\"\n565\t\t\t}\n566\t\t\tif len(sessions) > sessionHeight-2 {\n567\t\t\t\tsessionsPanel += dimStyle.Render(fmt.Sprintf(\"... and %d more\", len(sessions)-(sessionHeight-2)))\n568\t\t\t}\n569\t\t}\n570\t\n571\t\t// System Health Panel\n572\t\thealth := m.state.GetHealth()\n573\t\thealthPanel := headerStyle.Render(\"System Health\") + \"\\n\"\n574\t\tfor _, check := range health.Checks {\n575\t\t\tcheckStyle := successStyle\n576\t\t\tif !check.Healthy {\n577\t\t\t\tcheckStyle = errorStyle\n578\t\t\t}\n579\t\t\tmsg := check.Message\n580\t\t\tif msg == \"\" {\n581\t\t\t\tmsg = \"ok\"\n582\t\t\t}\n583\t\t\thealthPanel += fmt.Sprintf(\"%s %-12s %s\\n\",\n584\t\t\t\tcheckStyle.Render(statusIcon(check.Healthy)),\n585\t\t\t\tcheck.Name+\":\", checkStyle.Render(msg),\n586\t\t\t)\n587\t\t}\n588\t\n589\t\t// Messages In Flight Panel\n590\t\tlogEntries := m.state.GetMessageLog()\n591\t\tlogPanel := headerStyle.Render(fmt.Sprintf(\"Messages In Flight (%d active)\", m.state.GetActiveMessageCount())) + \"\\n\"\n592\t\tstartIdx := len(logEntries) - logHeight + 1\n593\t\tif startIdx < 0 {\n594\t\t\tstartIdx = 0\n595\t\t}\n596\t\tfor i := startIdx; i < len(logEntries); i++ {\n597\t\t\tlogPanel += dimStyle.Render(logEntries[i].Time+\" \") + logEntries[i].Msg + \"\\n\"\n598\t\t}\n599\t\n600\t\t// Command Log Panel\n601\t\tcmdEntries := m.state.GetCommandLog()\n602\t\tcmdPanel := headerStyle.Render(\"Command Log\") + \"\\n\"\n603\t\tcmdStartIdx := len(cmdEntries) - logHeight + 1\n604\t\tif cmdStartIdx < 0 {\n605\t\t\tcmdStartIdx = 0\n606\t\t}\n607\t\tfor i := cmdStartIdx; i < len(cmdEntries); i++ {\n608\t\t\tcmd := cmdEntries[i]\n609\t\t\tcmdStyle := successStyle\n610\t\t\tif !cmd.Success {\n611\t\t\t\tcmdStyle = errorStyle\n612\t\t\t}\n613\t\t\tcmdPanel += dimStyle.Render(cmd.Time+\" \") +\n614\t\t\t\tcmdStyle.Render(truncateString(cmd.Command, 30)) +\n615\t\t\t\tdimStyle.Render(fmt.Sprintf(\" uid=%d\", cmd.UserID)) + \"\\n\"\n616\t\t}\n617\t\n618\t\t// Cost Tracker Panel\n619\t\ttotalCost := m.state.GetTotalCost()\n620\t\tcostPanel := headerStyle.Render(\"Cost Tracker\") + \"\\n\"\n621\t\tcostPanel += fmt.Sprintf(\"Total Cost: $%.4f\\n\", totalCost)\n622\t\tif len(sessions) > 0 {\n623\t\t\tcostPanel += dimStyle.Render(\"Top sessions by cost:\\n\")\n624\t\t\t// Sort sessions by cost\n625\t\t\tsortedSessions := make([]*SessionInfo, len(sessions))\n626\t\t\tcopy(sortedSessions, sessions)\n627\t\t\tfor i := 0; i < len(sortedSessions)-1; i++ {\n628\t\t\t\tfor j := i + 1; j < len(sortedSessions); j++ {\n629\t\t\t\t\tif sortedSessions[j].TotalCostUSD > sortedSessions[i].TotalCostUSD {\n630\t\t\t\t\t\tsortedSessions[i], sortedSessions[j] = sortedSessions[j], sortedSessions[i]\n631\t\t\t\t\t}\n632\t\t\t\t}\n633\t\t\t}\n634\t\t\tfor i := 0; i < len(sortedSessions) && i < 5; i++ {\n635\t\t\t\tsess := sortedSessions[i]\n636\t\t\t\tcostPanel += fmt.Sprintf(\"  ch=%d th=%d: $%.4f\\n\", sess.ChatID, sess.ThreadID, sess.TotalCostUSD)\n637\t\t\t}\n638\t\t}\n639\t\n640\t\t// Layout\n641\t\tleftColumn := lipgloss.JoinVertical(lipgloss.Left,\n642\t\t\tpanelStyle.Width(leftWidth).Height(sessionHeight).Render(sessionsPanel),\n643\t\t\tpanelStyle.Width(leftWidth).Height(healthHeight).Render(healthPanel),\n644\t\t)\n645\t\trightColumn := lipgloss.JoinVertical(lipgloss.Left,\n646\t\t\tpanelStyle.Width(rightWidth).Height(logHeight).Render(logPanel),\n647\t\t\tpanelStyle.Width(rightWidth).Height(logHeight).Render(cmdPanel),\n648\t\t\tpanelStyle.Width(rightWidth).Height(6).Render(costPanel),\n649\t\t)\n650\t\n651\t\tmainContent := lipgloss.JoinHorizontal(lipgloss.Top, leftColumn, rightColumn)\n652\t\n653\t\treturn header + \"\\n\" + mainContent\n654\t}\n655\t\n656\t// statusIcon returns a checkmark or cross icon.\n657\tfunc statusIcon(healthy bool) string {\n658\t\tif healthy {\n659\t\t\treturn \"✓\"\n660\t\t}\n661\t\treturn \"✗\"\n662\t}\n663\t\n664\t// truncateString truncates a string to a maximum length.\n665\tfunc truncateString(s string, maxLen int) string {\n666\t\tif len(s) <= maxLen {\n667\t\t\treturn s\n668\t\t}\n669\t\treturn s[:maxLen-1] + \"…\"\n670\t}\n671\t\n672\t// connectCmd attempts to connect to the event socket.\n673\tfunc connectCmd() tea.Cmd {\n674\t\treturn func() tea.Msg {\n675\t\t\tconn, err := net.Dial(\"unix\", socketPath)\n676\t\t\tif err != nil {\n677\t\t\t\treturn disconnectedMsg{err: fmt.Errorf(\"dial failed: %w\", err)}\n678\t\t\t}\n679\t\t\treturn connectedMsg{Conn: conn}\n680\t\t}\n681\t}\n682\t\n683\t// waitForNextEvent waits for the next event on an existing connection.\n684\tfunc waitForNextEvent(conn net.Conn) tea.Cmd {\n685\t\treturn func() tea.Msg {\n686\t\t\tdecoder := json.NewDecoder(conn)\n687\t\t\tvar event Event\n688\t\t\tif err := decoder.Decode(&event); err != nil {\n689\t\t\t\tconn.Close()\n690\t\t\t\tif err == io.EOF {\n691\t\t\t\t\treturn disconnectedMsg{err: fmt.Errorf(\"connection closed\")}\n692\t\t\t\t}\n693\t\t\t\treturn disconnectedMsg{err: fmt.Errorf(\"decode failed: %w\", err)}\n694\t\t\t}\n695\t\t\treturn eventReceivedMsg(event)\n696\t\t}\n697\t}\n698\t\n699\t// readEventsCmd reads events from the socket.\n700\tfunc readEventsCmd() tea.Cmd {\n701\t\treturn func() tea.Msg {\n702\t\t\tconn, err := net.Dial(\"unix\", socketPath)\n703\t\t\tif err != nil {\n704\t\t\t\treturn disconnectedMsg{err: fmt.Errorf(\"dial failed: %w\", err)}\n705\t\t\t}\n706\t\n707\t\t\tdecoder := json.NewDecoder(conn)\n708\t\t\tvar event Event\n709\t\t\tif err := decoder.Decode(&event); err != nil {\n710\t\t\t\tconn.Close()\n711\t\t\t\tif err == io.EOF {\n712\t\t\t\t\treturn disconnectedMsg{err: fmt.Errorf(\"connection closed\")}\n713\t\t\t\t}\n714\t\t\t\treturn disconnectedMsg{err: fmt.Errorf(\"decode failed: %w\", err)}\n715\t\t\t}\n716\t\t\t// Store connection for subsequent reads\n717\t\t\treturn eventWithConnMsg{Event: event, Conn: conn}\n718\t\t}\n719\t}\n720\t\n721\t// tickCmd returns a command that sends tick messages periodically.\n722\tfunc tickCmd() tea.Cmd {\n723\t\treturn fmt.Errorf(\"begin migration %d: %w\", version, err)\n362\t\t\t}\n363\t\t\tif _, err := tx.Exec(ddl); err != nil {\n364\t\t\t\ttx.Rollback()\n365\t\t\t\treturn fmt.Errorf(\"apply migration %d: %w\", version, err)\n366\t\t\t}\n367\t\t\tif _, err := tx.Exec(\n368\t\t\t\t`INSERT INTO schema_version (version) VALUES (?)`, version,\n369\t\t\t); err != nil {\n370\t\t\t\ttx.Rollback()\n371\t\t\t\treturn fmt.Errorf(\"record migration %d: %w\", version, err)\n372\t\t\t}\n373\t\t\tif err := tx.Commit(); err != nil {\n374\t\t\t\treturn fmt.Errorf(\"commit migration %d: %w\", version, err)\n375\t\t\t}\n376\t\t}\n377\t\treturn nil\n378\t}\n379\t\n380\t// ── groups ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n381\t\n382\t// GetGroup returns the group with the given chat_id, or (nil, nil) if not found.\n383\tfunc (d *DB) GetGroup(ctx context.Context, chatID int64) (*Group, error) {\n384\t\trow := d.db.QueryRowContext(ctx,\n385\t\t\t`SELECT chat_id, COALESCE(name,''), cwd, default_model, max_budget, timeout_sec,\n386\t\t\t        COALESCE(permission_mode,'acceptEdits'),\n387\t\t\t        COALESCE(allowed_tools,''), COALESCE(disallowed_tools,''),\n388\t\t\t        COALESCE(max_subtasks,5), COALESCE(max_workers,5), COALESCE(progress_interval_sec,120),\n389\t\t\t        COALESCE(dispatcher_mode,1), created_at\n390\t\t\t FROM groups WHERE chat_id = ?`, chatID)\n391\t\treturn scanGroup(row)\n392\t}\n393\t\n394\t// UpsertGroup inserts or replaces a group record.\n395\tfunc (d *DB) UpsertGroup(ctx context.Context, g *Group) error {\n396\t\t_, err := d.db.ExecContext(ctx,\n397\t\t\t`INSERT INTO groups (chat_id, name, cwd, default_model, max_budget, timeout_sec, permission_mode, allowed_tools, disallowed_tools, max_subtasks, max_workers, progress_interval_sec, dispatcher_mode, created_at)\n398\t\t\t VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)\n399\t\t\t ON CONFLICT(chat_id) DO UPDATE SET\n400\t\t\t   name                = excluded.name,\n401\t\t\t   cwd                  = excluded.cwd,\n402\t\t\t   default_model       = excluded.default_model,\n403\t\t\t   max_budget          = excluded.max_budget,\n404\t\t\t   timeout_sec         = excluded.timeout_sec,\n405\t\t\t   permission_mode      = excluded.permission_mode,\n406\t\t\t   allowed_tools        = excluded.allowed_tools,\n407\t\t\t   disallowed_tools     = excluded.disallowed_tools,\n408\t\t\t   max_subtasks         = excluded.max_subtasks,\n409\t\t\t   max_workers          = excluded.max_workers,\n410\t\t\t   progress_interval_sec = excluded.progress_interval_sec,\n411\t\t\t   dispatcher_mode      = excluded.dispatcher_mode`,\n412\t\t\tg.ChatID, g.Name, g.CWD, g.DefaultModel, g.MaxBudget, g.TimeoutSec, g.PermissionMode,\n413\t\t\tnullableString(g.AllowedTools), nullableString(g.DisallowedTools),\n414\t\t\tg.MaxSubtasks, g.MaxWorkers, g.ProgressIntervalSec, g.DispatcherMode,\n415\t\t\tg.CreatedAt.UTC().Format(time.RFC3339),\n416\t\t)\n417\t\treturn err\n418\t}\n419\t\n420\t// ListGroups returns all configured groups.\n421\tfunc (d *DB) ListGroups(ctx context.Context) ([]*Group, error) {\n422\t\trows, err := d.db.QueryContext(ctx,\n423\t\t\t`SELECT chat_id, COALESCE(name,''), cwd, default_model, max_budget, timeout_sec,\n424\t\t\t        COALESCE(permission_mode,'acceptEdits'),\n425\t\t\t        COALESCE(allowed_tools,''), COALESCE(disallowed_tools,''),\n426\t\t\t        COALESCE(max_subtasks,5), COALESCE(max_workers,5), COALESCE(progress_interval_sec,120),\n427\t\t\t        COALESCE(dispatcher_mode,1), created_at\n428\t\t\t FROM groups ORDER BY created_at`)\n429\t\tif err != nil {\n430\t\t\treturn nil, err\n431\t\t}\n432\t\tdefer rows.Close()\n433\t\n434\t\tvar groups []*Group\n435\t\tfor rows.Next() {\n436\t\t\tg, err := scanGroup(rows)\n437\t\t\tif err != nil {\n438\t\t\t\treturn nil, err\n439\t\t\t}\n440\t\t\tgroups = append(groups, g)\n441\t\t}\n442\t\treturn groups, rows.Err()\n443\t}\n444\t\n445\t// DeleteGroup removes a group and all its sessions (cascade handled manually).\n446\tfunc (d *DB) DeleteGroup(ctx context.Context, chatID int64) error {\n447\t\ttx, err := d.db.BeginTx(ctx, nil)\n448\t\tif err != nil {\n449\t\t\treturn err\n450\t\t}\n451\t\tdefer tx.Rollback()\n452\t\n453\t\tif _, err := tx.ExecContext(ctx, `DELETE FROM sessions WHERE chat_id = ?`, chatID); err != nil {\n454\t\t\treturn err\n455\t\t}\n456\t\tif _, err := tx.ExecContext(ctx, `DELETE FROM sent_messages WHERE chat_id = ?`, chatID); err != nil {\n457\t\t\treturn err\n458\t\t}\n459\t\tif _, err := tx.ExecContext(ctx, `DELETE FROM groups WHERE chat_id = ?`, chatID); err != nil {\n460\t\t\treturn err\n461\t\t}\n462\t\treturn tx.Commit()\n463\t}\n464\t\n465\ttype groupScanner interface {\n466\t\tScan(dest ...any) error\n467\t}\n468\t\n469\tfunc scanGroup(s groupScanner) (*Group, error) {\n470\t\tvar g Group\n471\t\tvar createdAt string\n472\t\terr := s.Scan(&g.ChatID, &g.Name, &g.CWD, &g.DefaultModel, &g.MaxBudget, &g.TimeoutSec, &g.PermissionMode, &g.AllowedTools, &g.DisallowedTools, &g.MaxSubtasks, &g.MaxWorkers, &g.ProgressIntervalSec, &g.DispatcherMode, &createdAt)\n473\t\tif err == sql.ErrNoRows {\n474\t\t\treturn nil, nil\n475\t\t}\n476\t\tif err != nil {\n477\t\t\treturn nil, err\n478\t\t}\n479\t\tg.CreatedAt, _ = time.Parse(time.RFC3339, createdAt)\n480\t\treturn &g, nil\n481\t}\n482\t\n483\t// ── sessions ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n484\t\n485\t// GetSession returns the session for (chatID, threadID), or (nil, nil) if not found.\n486\tfunc (d *DB) GetSession(ctx context.Context, chatID, threadID int64) (*Session, error) {\n487\t\trow := d.db.QueryRowContext(ctx,\n488\t\t\t`SELECT chat_id, thread_id, session_id, cwd, COALESCE(model,''), status,\n489\t\t\t        created_at, last_active, message_count, icon_color, pinned_message_id, total_cost_usd,\n490\t\t\t        COALESCE(summary,''), COALESCE(notification_mode,'live'), timeout_sec, COALESCE(dispatcher_mode,-1)\n491\t\t\t FROM sessions WHERE chat_id = ? AND thread_id = ?`, chatID, threadID)\n492\t\treturn scanSession(row)\n493\t}\n494\t\n495\t// CreateSession inserts a new session record.\n496\tfunc (d *DB) CreateSession(ctx context.Context, s *Session) error {\n497\t\tnow := time.Now().UTC()\n498\t\tif s.CreatedAt.IsZero() {\n499\t\t\ts.CreatedAt = now\n500\t\t}\n501\t\tif s.LastActive.IsZero() {\n502\t\t\ts.LastActive = now\n503\t\t}\n504\t\tif s.Status == \"\" {\n505\t\t\ts.Status = \"active\"\n506\t\t}\n507\t\tif s.IconColor == 0 {\n508\t\t\ts.IconColor = 7322096 // Default to light blue (0x6FB9F0)\n509\t\t}\n510\t\tif s.NotificationMode == \"\" {\n511\t\t\ts.NotificationMode = \"live\"\n512\t\t}\n513\t\t_, err := d.db.ExecContext(ctx,\n514\t\t\t`INSERT INTO sessions\n515\t\t\t   (chat_id, thread_id, session_id, cwd, model, status, icon_color, created_at, last_active, message_count, pinned_message_id, total_cost_usd, summary, notification_mode, timeout_sec)\n516\t\t\t VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)`,\n517\t\t\ts.ChatID, s.ThreadID, s.SessionID, s.CWD, nullableString(s.Model), s.Status,\n518\t\t\ts.IconColor,\n519\t\t\ts.CreatedAt.UTC().Format(time.RFC3339),\n520\t\t\ts.LastActive.UTC().Format(time.RFC3339),\n521\t\t\ts.MessageCount,\n522\t\t\ts.PinnedMessageID,\n523\t\t\ts.TotalCostUSD,\n524\t\t\tnullableString(s.Summary),\n525\t\t\ts.NotificationMode,\n526\t\t\ts.TimeoutSec,\n527\t\t)\n528\t\treturn err\n529\t}\n530\t\n531\t// UpdateSession updates mutable fields on an existing session.\n532\tfunc (d *DB) UpdateSession(ctx context.Context, s *Session) error {\n533\t\t_, err := d.db.ExecContext(ctx,\n534\t\t\t`UPDATE sessions\n535\t\t\t SET session_id    = ?,\n536\t\t\t     cwd           = ?,\n537\t\t\t     model         = ?,\n538\t\t\t     status        = ?,\n539\t\t\t     icon_color    = ?,\n540\t\t\t     last_active   = ?,\n541\t\t\t     message_count = ?,\n542\t\t\t     pinned_message_id = ?,\n543\t\t\t     total_cost_usd    = ?,\n544\t\t\t     summary           = ?,\n545\t\t\t     notification_mode = ?,\n546\t\t\t     timeout_sec       = ?,\n547\t\t\t     dispatcher_mode   = ?\n548\t\t\t WHERE chat_id = ? AND thread_id = ?`,\n549\t\t\ts.SessionID, s.CWD, nullableString(s.Model), s.Status,\n550\t\t\ts.IconColor,\n551\t\t\ts.LastActive.UTC().Format(time.RFC3339),\n552\t\t\ts.MessageCount,\n553\t\t\ts.PinnedMessageID,\n554\t\t\ts.TotalCostUSD,\n555\t\t\tnullableString(s.Summary),\n556\t\t\ts.NotificationMode,\n557\t\t\ts.TimeoutSec,\n558\t\t\ts.DispatcherMode,\n559\t\t\ts.ChatID, s.ThreadID,\n560\t\t)\n561\t\treturn err\n562\t}\n563\t\n564\t// SetSessionIconColor updates only the icon_color field for a session.\n565\tfunc (d *DB) SetSessionIconColor(ctx context.Context, chatID, threadID int64, color int) error {\n566\t\t_, err := d.db.ExecContext(ctx,\n567\t\t\t`UPDATE sessions SET icon_color = ? AND thread_id = ?`,\n765\t\t\tchatID, threadID)\n766\t\terr := row.Scan(&total)\n767\t\treturn total, err\n768\t}\n769\t\n770\t// TopicCostSummary holds cost breakdown for a single topic.\n771\ttype TopicCostSummary struct {\n772\t\tThreadID    int64\n773\t\tTotalCost   float64\n774\t\tEventCount  int\n775\t}\n776\t\n777\t// GetCostsByTopic returns a list of topics with their costs for a group.\n778\tfunc (d *DB) GetCostsByTopic(ctx context.Context, chatID int64) ([]*TopicCostSummary, error) {\n779\t\trows, err := d.db.QueryContext(ctx,\n780\t\t\t`SELECT thread_id,\n781\t\t\t        COALESCE(SUM(cost_usd), 0) as total_cost,\n782\t\t\t        COUNT(*) as event_count\n783\t\t\t FROM cost_events\n784\t\t\t WHERE chat_id = ?\n785\t\t\t GROUP BY thread_id\n786\t\t\t ORDER BY total_cost DESC`,\n787\t\t\tchatID)\n788\t\tif err != nil {\n789\t\t\treturn nil, err\n790\t\t}\n791\t\tdefer rows.Close()\n792\t\n793\t\tvar summaries []*TopicCostSummary\n794\t\tfor rows.Next() {\n795\t\t\tvar s TopicCostSummary\n796\t\t\tif err := rows.Scan(&s.ThreadID, &s.TotalCost, &s.EventCount); err != nil {\n797\t\t\t\treturn nil, err\n798\t\t\t}\n799\t\t\tsummaries = append(summaries, &s)\n800\t\t}\n801\t\treturn summaries, rows.Err()\n802\t}\n803\t\n804\t// DailyCostSummary holds cost data for a single day.\n805\ttype DailyCostSummary struct {\n806\t\tDate    string\n807\t\tTotalCost float64\n808\t}\n809\t\n810\t// GetDailyCosts returns daily cost totals for a group within the last N days.\n811\tfunc (d *DB) GetDailyCosts(ctx context.Context, chatID int64, days int) ([]*DailyCostSummary, error) {\n812\t\trows, err := d.db.QueryContext(ctx,\n813\t\t\t`SELECT DATE(created_at) as date,\n814\t\t\t        COALESCE(SUM(cost_usd), 0) as total_cost\n815\t\t\t FROM cost_events\n816\t\t\t WHERE chat_id = ?\n817\t\t\t   AND DATE(created_at) >= DATE('now', '-' || ? || ' days')\n818\t\t\t GROUP BY DATE(created_at)\n819\t\t\t ORDER BY date DESC`,\n820\t\t\tchatID, days)\n821\t\tif err != nil {\n822\t\t\treturn nil, err\n823\t\t}\n824\t\tdefer rows.Close()\n825\t\n826\t\tvar summaries []*DailyCostSummary\n827\t\tfor rows.Next() {\n828\t\t\tvar s DailyCostSummary\n829\t\t\tif err := rows.Scan(&s.Date, &s.TotalCost); err != nil {\n830\t\t\t\treturn nil, err\n831\t\t\t}\n832\t\t\tsummaries = append(summaries, &s)\n833\t\t}\n834\t\treturn summaries, rows.Err()\n835\t}\n836\t\n837\t// UpdateSessionSummary updates the summary field for a session.\n838\tfunc (d *DB) UpdateSessionSummary(ctx context.Context, chatID, threadID int64, summary string) error {\n839\t\t_, err := d.db.ExecContext(ctx,\n840\t\t\t`UPDATE sessions SET summary = ? INTEGER NOT NULL,\n\t\tapplied_at TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT (datetime('now'))\n\t);`)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn fmt.Errorf(\"create schema_version: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\n\tvar current int\n\trow := tx.Exec(\n\t\t\t`INSERT INTO schema_version (version) VALUES (?)`, version,\n\t\t); err != nil {\n\t\t\ttx.Rollback()\n\t\t\treturn fmt.Errorf(\"record migration %d: %w\", version, err)\n\t\t}\n\t\tif replaces a group record.\nfunc (d *DB) UpsertGroup(ctx context.Context, g *Group) error {\n\t_, err := d.db.ExecContext(ctx,\n\t\t`INSERT INTO groups (chat_id, name, cwd, default_model, max_budget, timeout_sec, permission_mode, allowed_tools, disallowed_tools, max_subtasks, max_workers, progress_interval_sec, dispatcher_mode, created_at)\n\t\t VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)\n\t\t ON CONFLICT(chat_id) DO UPDATE SET\n\t\t   name                = excluded.name,\n\t\t   cwd                  = excluded.cwd,\n\t\t   default_model       = excluded.default_model,\n\t\t   max_budget          = excluded.max_budget,\n\t\t   timeout_sec         = excluded.timeout_sec,\n\t\t   permission_mode      = excluded.permission_mode,\n\t\t   allowed_tools        = excluded.allowed_tools,\n\t\t   disallowed_tools     = excluded.disallowed_tools,\n\t\t   max_subtasks         = excluded.max_subtasks,\n\t\t   max_workers          = excluded.max_workers,\n\t\t   progress_interval_sec = excluded.progress_interval_sec,\n\t\t   dispatcher_mode      = excluded.dispatcher_mode`,\n\t\tg.ChatID, g.Name, g.CWD, g.DefaultModel, g.MaxBudget, g.TimeoutSec, g.PermissionMode,\n\t\tnullableString(g.AllowedTools), nullableString(g.DisallowedTools),\n\t\tg.MaxSubtasks, g.MaxWorkers, g.ProgressIntervalSec, g.DispatcherMode,\n\t\tg.CreatedAt.UTC().Format(time.RFC3339),\n\t)\n\treturn err\n}\n\n// ListGroups returns all configured groups.\nfunc (d *DB) ListGroups(ctx context.Context) ([]*Group, error) {\n\trows, err := d.db.QueryContext(ctx,\n\t\t`SELECT chat_id, COALESCE(name,''), cwd, default_model, max_budget, timeout_sec,\n\t\t        COALESCE(permission_mode,'acceptEdits'),\n\t\t        COALESCE(allowed_tools,''), COALESCE(disallowed_tools,''),\n\t\t        COALESCE(max_subtasks,5), COALESCE(max_workers,5), COALESCE(progress_interval_sec,120),\n\t\t        COALESCE(dispatcher_mode,1), created_at\n\t\t FROM groups ORDER BY created_at`)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn nil, err\n\t}\n\tdefer rows.Close()\n\n\tvar groups []*Group\n\tfor rows.Next() {\n\t\tg, err := scanGroup(rows)\n\t\tif err != nil {\n\t\t\treturn nil, err\n\t\t}\n\t\tgroups = append(groups, g)\n\t}\n\treturn groups, rows.Err()\n}\n\n// DeleteGroup removes a group and all its sessions (cascade handled manually).\nfunc (d *DB) DeleteGroup(ctx context.Context, chatID int64) error {\n\ttx, err := d.db.BeginTx(ctx, nil)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn err\n\t}\n\tdefer tx.Rollback()\n\n\tif _, err := tx.ExecContext(ctx, `DELETE FROM sessions WHERE chat_id = ?`, chatID); err != nil {\n\t\treturn err\n\t}\n\tif _, err := tx.ExecContext(ctx, `DELETE FROM sent_messages WHERE chat_id = ?`, chatID); err != nil {\n\t\treturn err\n\t}\n\tif _, err := tx.ExecContext(ctx, `DELETE FROM groups WHERE chat_id = ?`, chatID); err != nil {\n\t\treturn err\n\t}\n\treturn tx.Commit()\n}\n\ntype groupScanner interface {\n\tScan(dest ...any) error\n}\n\nfunc scanGroup(s groupScanner) (*Group, error) {\n\tvar g Group\n\tvar createdAt string\n\terr := s.Scan(&g.ChatID, &g.Name, &g.CWD, &g.DefaultModel, &g.MaxBudget, &g.TimeoutSec, &g.PermissionMode, &g.AllowedTools, &g.DisallowedTools, &g.MaxSubtasks, &g.MaxWorkers, &g.ProgressIntervalSec, &g.DispatcherMode, &createdAt)\n\tif err == sql.ErrNoRows {\n\t\treturn nil, nil\n\t}\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn nil, err\n\t}\n\tg.CreatedAt, _ = time.Parse(time.RFC3339, createdAt)\n\treturn &g, nil\n}\n\n// ── sessions ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n\n// GetSession returns the session for (chatID, threadID), or (nil, nil) if not found.\nfunc (d *DB) GetSession(ctx context.Context, chatID, threadID int64) (*Session, error) {\n\trow := d.db.QueryRowContext(ctx,\n\t\t`SELECT chat_id, thread_id, session_id, cwd, COALESCE(model,''), status,\n\t\t        created_at, last_active, message_count, icon_color, pinned_message_id, total_cost_usd,\n\t\t        COALESCE(summary,''), COALESCE(notification_mode,'live'), timeout_sec, COALESCE(dispatcher_mode,-1)\n\t\t FROM sessions WHERE chat_id = ? AND thread_id = ?`, chatID, threadID)\n\treturn scanSession(row)\n}\n\n// CreateSession inserts a new session record.\nfunc (d *DB) CreateSession(ctx context.Context, s *Session) error {\n\tnow := time.Now().UTC()\n\tif s.CreatedAt.IsZero() {\n\t\ts.CreatedAt = now\n\t}\n\tif s.LastActive.IsZero() {\n\t\ts.LastActive = now\n\t}\n\tif s.Status == \"\" {\n\t\ts.Status = \"active\"\n\t}\n\tif s.IconColor == 0 {\n\t\ts.IconColor = 7322096 // Default to light blue (0x6FB9F0)\n\t}\n\tif s.NotificationMode == \"\" {\n\t\ts.NotificationMode = \"live\"\n\t}\n\t_, err := d.db.ExecContext(ctx,\n\t\t`INSERT INTO sessions\n\t\t   (chat_id, thread_id, session_id, cwd, model, status, icon_color, created_at, last_active, message_count, pinned_message_id, total_cost_usd, summary, notification_mode, timeout_sec)\n\t\t VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)`,\n\t\ts.ChatID, s.ThreadID, s.SessionID, s.CWD, nullableString(s.Model), s.Status,\n\t\ts.IconColor,\n\t\ts.CreatedAt.UTC().Format(time.RFC3339),\n\t\ts.LastActive.UTC().Format(time.RFC3339),\n\t\ts.MessageCount,\n\t\ts.PinnedMessageID,\n\t\ts.TotalCostUSD,\n\t\tnullableString(s.Summary),\n\t\ts.NotificationMode,\n\t\ts.TimeoutSec,\n\t)\n\treturn err\n}\n\n// UpdateSession updates mutable fields on an existing session.\nfunc (d *DB) UpdateSession(ctx context.Context, s *Session) error {\n\t_, err := d.db.ExecContext(ctx,\n\t\t`UPDATE sessions\n\t\t SET session_id    = ?,\n\t\t     cwd           = ?,\n\t\t     model         = ?,\n\t\t     status        = ?,\n\t\t     icon_color    = ?,\n\t\t     last_active   = ?,\n\t\t     message_count = ?,\n\t\t     pinned_message_id = ?,\n\t\t     total_cost_usd    = ?,\n\t\t     summary           = ?,\n\t\t     notification_mode = ?,\n\t\t     timeout_sec       = ?,\n\t\t     dispatcher_mode   = ?\n\t\t WHERE chat_id = ? AND thread_id = ?`,\n\t\ts.SessionID, s.CWD, nullableString(s.Model), s.Status,\n\t\ts.IconColor,\n\t\ts.LastActive.UTC().Format(time.RFC3339),\n\t\ts.MessageCount,\n\t\ts.PinnedMessageID,\n\t\ts.TotalCostUSD,\n\t\tnullableString(s.Summary),\n\t\ts.NotificationMode,\n\t\ts.TimeoutSec,\n\t\ts.DispatcherMode,\n\t\ts.ChatID, s.ThreadID,\n\t)\n\treturn err\n}\n\n// SetSessionIconColor updates only the icon_color field for a session.\nfunc (d *DB) SetSessionIconColor(ctx context.Context, chatID, threadID int64, color int) error {\n\t_, err := d.db.ExecContext(ctx,\n\t\t`UPDATE sessions SET icon_color = ? WHERE chat_id = ? AND thread_id = ?`,\n\t\tcolor, chatID, threadID,\n\t)\n\treturn err\n}\n\n// SetSessionNotificationMode updates only the notification_mode field for a session.\n// Valid modes are: \"live\" (default), \"summary\", \"quiet\".\nfunc (d *DB) SetSessionNotificationMode(ctx context.Context, chatID, threadID int64, mode string) error {\n\t_, err := d.db.ExecContext(ctx,\n\t\t`UPDATE sessions SET notification_mode = ? WHERE chat_id = ? AND thread_id = ?`,\n\t\tmode, chatID, threadID,\n\t)\n\treturn err\n}\n\n// SetSessionDispatcherMode updates only the dispatcher_mode field for a session.\n// Valid values: 1 (enabled), 0 (disabled), -1 (use group default).\nfunc (d *DB) SetSessionDispatcherMode(ctx context.Context, chatID, threadID int64, mode int) error {\n\t_, err := d.db.ExecContext(ctx,\n\t\t`UPDATE sessions SET dispatcher_mode = ? WHERE chat_id = ? AND thread_id = ?`,\n\t\tmode, chatID, threadID,\n\t)\n\treturn err\n}\n\n// GetSessionIconColor returns the current icon_color for a session.\n// Returns the default active color if session not found.\nfunc (d *DB) GetSessionIconColor(ctx context.Context, chatID, threadID int64) int {\n\tsession, err := d.GetSession(ctx, chatID, threadID)\n\tif err != nil || session == nil {\n\t\treturn ColorActive\n\t}\n\treturn session.IconColor\n}\n\n// TouchSession bumps last_active and increments message_count atomically.\nfunc (d *DB) TouchSession(ctx context.Context, chatID, threadID int64) error {\n\t_, err := d.db.ExecContext(ctx,\n\t\t`UPDATE sessions\n\t\t SET last_active   = datetime('now'),\n\t\t     message_count = message_count + 1\n\t\t WHERE chat_id = ? AND thread_id = ?`,\n\t\tchatID, threadID,\n\t)\n\treturn err\n}\n\n// ListSessions returns all sessions for a group, ordered by last_active descending.\nfunc (d *DB) ListSessions(ctx context.Context, chatID int64) ([]*Session, error) {\n\trows, err := d.db.QueryContext(ctx,\n\t\t`SELECT chat_id, thread_id, session_id, cwd, COALESCE(model,''), status,\n\t\t        created_at, last_active, message_count, icon_color, pinned_message_id, total_cost_usd,\n\t\t        COALESCE(summary,''), COALESCE(notification_mode,'live'), timeout_sec, COALESCE(dispatcher_mode,-1)\n\t\t FROM sessions WHERE chat_id = ? ORDER BY last_active DESC`, chatID)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn nil, err\n\t}\n\tdefer rows.Close()\n\n\tvar sessions []*Session\n\tfor rows.Next() {\n\t\ts, err := scanSession(rows)\n\t\tif err != nil {\n\t\t\treturn nil, err\n\t\t}\n\t\tsessions = append(sessions, s)\n\t}\n\treturn sessions, rows.Err()\n}\n\n// ListAllSessions returns all sessions across all groups, ordered by last_active descending.\nfunc (d *DB) ListAllSessions(ctx context.Context) ([]*Session, error) {\n\trows, err := d.db.QueryContext(ctx,\n\t\t`SELECT chat_id, thread_id, session_id, cwd, COALESCE(model,''), status,\n\t\t        created_at, last_active, message_count, icon_color, pinned_message_id, total_cost_usd,\n\t\t        COALESCE(summary,''), COALESCE(notification_mode,'live'), timeout_sec, COALESCE(dispatcher_mode,-1)\n\t\t FROM sessions ORDER BY last_active DESC`)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn nil, err\n\t}\n\tdefer rows.Close()\n\n\tvar sessions []*Session\n\tfor rows.Next() {\n\t\ts, err := scanSession(rows)\n\t\tif err != nil {\n\t\t\treturn nil, err\n\t\t}\n\t\tsessions = append(sessions, s)\n\t}\n\treturn sessions, rows.Err()\n}\n\n// CloseSession marks a session as closed.\nfunc (d *DB) CloseSession(ctx context.Context, chatID, threadID int64) error {\n\t_, err := d.db.ExecContext(ctx,\n\t\t`UPDATE sessions SET status = 'closed' WHERE chat_id = ? AND thread_id = ?`,\n\t\tchatID, threadID)\n\treturn err\n}\n\n// DeleteSession removes a session record.\nfunc (d *DB) DeleteSession(ctx context.Context, chatID, threadID int64) error {\n\t_, err := d.db.ExecContext(ctx,\n\t\t`DELETE FROM sessions WHERE chat_id = ? AND thread_id = ?`, chatID, threadID)\n\treturn err\n}\n\n// SetSessionPinnedMessageID updates the pinned_message_id for a session.\nfunc (d *DB) SetSessionPinnedMessageID(ctx context.Context, chatID, threadID int64, messageID int64) error {\n\t_, err := d.db.ExecContext(ctx,\n\t\t`UPDATE sessions SET pinned_message_id = ? WHERE chat_id = ? AND thread_id = ?`,\n\t\tmessageID, chatID, threadID,\n\t)\n\treturn err\n}\n\n// ListStaleSessions returns sessions where last_active is older than ttl.\n// Only returns sessions with status='active' — already inactive/closed sessions are excluded.\nfunc (d *DB) ListStaleSessions(ctx context.Context, ttl time.Duration) ([]*Session, error) {\n\trows, err := d.db.QueryContext(ctx,\n\t\t`SELECT chat_id, thread_id, session_id, cwd, COALESCE(model,''), status,\n\t\t\t        created_at, last_active, message_count, icon_color, pinned_message_id, total_cost_usd,\n\t\t\t        COALESCE(summary,''), COALESCE(notification_mode,'live'), timeout_sec, COALESCE(dispatcher_mode,-1)\n\t\t\t FROM sessions\n\t\t\t WHERE status = 'active'\n\t\t\t   AND datetime(last_active) < datetime('now', '-' || ? || ' seconds')\n\t\t\t ORDER BY last_active ASC`,\n\t\tint64(ttl.Seconds()),\n\t)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn nil, err\n\t}\n\tdefer rows.Close()\n\n\tvar sessions []*Session\n\tfor rows.Next() {\n\t\ts, err := scanSession(rows)\n\t\tif err != nil {\n\t\t\treturn nil, err\n\t\t}\n\t\tsessions = append(sessions, s)\n\t}\n\treturn sessions, rows.Err()\n}\n\n// SetSessionStatus updates the status field for a session.\nfunc (d *DB) SetSessionStatus(ctx context.Context, chatID, threadID int64, status string) error {\n\t_, err := d.db.ExecContext(ctx,\n\t\t`UPDATE sessions SET status = ? WHERE chat_id = ? AND thread_id = ?`,\n\t\tstatus, chatID, threadID,\n\t)\n\treturn err\n}\n\n// UpdateSessionCost adds to the total_cost_usd for a session.\nfunc (d *DB) UpdateSessionCost(ctx context.Context, chatID, threadID int64, costUSD float64) error {\n\t_, err := d.db.ExecContext(ctx,\n\t\t`UPDATE sessions SET total_cost_usd = total_cost_usd + ? WHERE chat_id = ? AND thread_id = ?`,\n\t\tcostUSD, chatID, threadID,\n\t)\n\treturn err\n}\n\n// ── cost_events ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n\n// RecordCostEvent inserts a new cost event record.\nfunc (d *DB) RecordCostEvent(ctx context.Context, e *CostEvent) error {\n\tif e.CreatedAt.IsZero() {\n\t\te.CreatedAt = time.Now().UTC()\n\t}\n\t_, err := d.db.ExecContext(ctx,\n\t\t`INSERT INTO cost_events\n\t\t   (chat_id, thread_id, cost_usd, input_tokens, output_tokens,\n\t\t    cache_read_tokens, cache_creation_tokens, model, created_at)\n\t\t VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)`,\n\t\te.ChatID, e.ThreadID, e.CostUSD, e.InputTokens, e.OutputTokens,\n\t\te.CacheReadTokens, e.CacheCreationTokens, nullableString(e.Model),\n\t\te.CreatedAt.UTC().Format(time.RFC3339),\n\t)\n\treturn err\n}\n\n// GetGroupTotalCost returns the sum of all costs for a group (all threads).\nfunc (d *DB) GetGroupTotalCost(ctx context.Context, chatID int64) (float64, error) {\n\tvar total float64\n\trow := d.db.QueryRowContext(ctx,\n\t\t`SELECT COALESCE(SUM(cost_usd), 0) FROM cost_events WHERE chat_id = ?`, chatID)\n\terr := row.Scan(&total)\n\treturn total, err\n}\n\n// GetTopicTotalCost returns the sum of all costs for a specific topic/thread.\nfunc (d *DB) GetTopicTotalCost(ctx context.Context, chatID, threadID int64) (float64, error) {\n\tvar total float64\n\trow := d.db.QueryRowContext(ctx,\n\t\t`SELECT COALESCE(SUM(cost_usd), 0) FROM cost_events WHERE chat_id = ? AND thread_id = ?`,\n\t\tchatID, threadID)\n\terr := row.Scan(&total)\n\treturn total, err\n}\n\n// TopicCostSummary holds cost breakdown for a single topic.\ntype TopicCostSummary struct {\n\tThreadID    int64\n\tTotalCost   float64\n\tEventCount  int\n}\n\n// GetCostsByTopic returns a list of topics with their costs for a group.\nfunc (d *DB) GetCostsByTopic(ctx context.Context, chatID int64) ([]*TopicCostSummary, error) {\n\trows, err := d.db.QueryContext(ctx,\n\t\t`SELECT thread_id,\n\t\t        COALESCE(SUM(cost_usd), 0) as total_cost,\n\t\t        COUNT(*) as event_count\n\t\t FROM cost_events\n\t\t WHERE chat_id = ?\n\t\t GROUP BY thread_id\n\t\t ORDER BY total_cost DESC`,\n\t\tchatID)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn nil, err\n\t}\n\tdefer rows.Close()\n\n\tvar summaries []*TopicCostSummary\n\tfor rows.Next() {\n\t\tvar s TopicCostSummary\n\t\tif err := rows.Scan(&s.ThreadID, &s.TotalCost, &s.EventCount); err != nil {\n\t\t\treturn nil, err\n\t\t}\n\t\tsummaries = append(summaries, &s)\n\t}\n\treturn summaries, rows.Err()\n}\n\n// DailyCostSummary holds cost data for a single day.\ntype DailyCostSummary struct {\n\tDate    string\n\tTotalCost float64\n}\n\n// GetDailyCosts returns daily cost totals for a group within the last N days.\nfunc (d *DB) GetDailyCosts(ctx context.Context, chatID int64, days int) ([]*DailyCostSummary, error) {\n\trows, err := d.db.QueryContext(ctx,\n\t\t`SELECT DATE(created_at) as date,\n\t\t        COALESCE(SUM(cost_usd), 0) as total_cost\n\t\t FROM cost_events\n\t\t WHERE chat_id = ?\n\t\t   AND DATE(created_at) >= DATE('now', '-' || ? || ' days')\n\t\t GROUP BY DATE(created_at)\n\t\t ORDER BY date DESC`,\n\t\tchatID, days)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn nil, err\n\t}\n\tdefer rows.Close()\n\n\tvar summaries []*DailyCostSummary\n\tfor rows.Next() {\n\t\tvar s DailyCostSummary\n\t\tif err := rows.Scan(&s.Date, &s.TotalCost); err != nil {\n\t\t\treturn nil, err\n\t\t}\n\t\tsummaries = append(summaries, &s)\n\t}\n\treturn summaries, rows.Err()\n}\n\n// UpdateSessionSummary updates the summary field for a session.\nfunc (d *DB) UpdateSessionSummary(ctx context.Context, chatID, threadID int64, summary string) error {\n\t_, err := d.db.ExecContext(ctx,\n\t\t`UPDATE sessions SET summary = ? WHERE chat_id = ? AND thread_id = ?`,\n\t\tnullableString(summary), chatID, threadID,\n\t)\n\treturn err\n}\n\ntype sessionScanner interface {\n\tScan(dest ...any) error\n}\n\nfunc scanSession(s sessionScanner) (*Session, error) {\n\tvar sess Session\n\tvar createdAt, lastActive string\n\terr := s.Scan(\n\t\t&sess.ChatID, &sess.ThreadID, &sess.SessionID, &sess.CWD,\n\t\t&sess.Model, &sess.Status, &createdAt, &lastActive, &sess.MessageCount, &sess.IconColor,\n\t\t&sess.PinnedMessageID, &sess.TotalCostUSD, &sess.Summary, &sess.NotificationMode, &sess.TimeoutSec,\n\t\t&sess.DispatcherMode,\n\t)\n\tif err == sql.ErrNoRows {\n\t\treturn nil, nil\n\t}\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn nil, err\n\t}\n\tsess.CreatedAt, _ = time.Parse(time.RFC3339, createdAt)\n\tsess.LastActive, _ = time.Parse(time.RFC3339, lastActive)\n\tif sess.NotificationMode == \"\" {\n\t\tsess.NotificationMode = \"live\"\n\t}\n\treturn &sess, nil\n}\n\n// ── allowed_users ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n\n// IsUserAllowed returns true if userID is in the allowed_users table.\nfunc (d *DB) IsUserAllowed(ctx context.Context, userID int64) (bool, error) {\n\tvar count int\n\terr := d.db.QueryRowContext(ctx,\n\t\t`SELECT COUNT(*) FROM allowed_users WHERE user_id = ?`, userID,\n\t).Scan(&count)\n\treturn count > 0, err\n}\n\n// GetUserRole returns the role for a user (\"admin\" or \"user\").\n// Returns empty string if user not found.\nfunc (d *DB) GetUserRole(ctx context.Context, userID int64) (string, error) {\n\tvar role string\n\terr := d.db.QueryRowContext(ctx,\n\t\t`SELECT role FROM allowed_users WHERE user_id = ?`, userID,\n\t).Scan(&role)\n\tif err == sql.ErrNoRows {\n\t\treturn \"\", nil\n\t}\n\treturn role, err\n}\n\n// IsUserAdmin returns true if the user has an \"admin\" role.\nfunc (d *DB) IsUserAdmin(ctx context.Context, userID int64) (bool, error) {\n\trole, err := d.GetUserRole(ctx, userID)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn false, err\n\t}\n\treturn role == \"admin\", nil\n}\n\n// GetAllowedUser returns the allowed user record, or (nil, nil) if not found.\nfunc (d *DB) GetAllowedUser(ctx context.Context, userID int64) (*AllowedUser, error) {\n\trow := d.db.QueryRowContext(ctx,\n\t\t`SELECT user_id, role, added_at FROM allowed_users WHERE user_id = ?`, userID)\n\n\tvar u AllowedUser\n\tvar addedAt string\n\terr := row.Scan(&u.UserID, &u.Role, &addedAt)\n\tif err == sql.ErrNoRows {\n\t\treturn nil, nil\n\t}\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn nil, err\n\t}\n\tu.AddedAt, _ = time.Parse(time.RFC3339, addedAt)\n\treturn &u, nil\n}\n\n// UpsertAllowedUser inserts or updates an allowed user.\nfunc (d *DB) UpsertAllowedUser(ctx context.Context, u *AllowedUser) error {\n\tif u.AddedAt.IsZero() {\n\t\tu.AddedAt = time.Now().UTC()\n\t}\n\tif u.Role == \"\" {\n\t\tu.Role = \"user\"\n\t}\n\t_, err := d.db.ExecContext(ctx,\n\t\t`INSERT INTO allowed_users (user_id, role, added_at) VALUES (?, ?, ?)\n\t\t ON CONFLICT(user_id) DO UPDATE SET role = excluded.role`,\n\t\tu.UserID, u.Role, u.AddedAt.UTC().Format(time.RFC3339),\n\t)\n\treturn err\n}\n\n// DeleteAllowedUser removes a user from the allow list.\nfunc (d *DB) DeleteAllowedUser(ctx context.Context, userID int64) error {\n\t_, err := d.db.ExecContext(ctx,\n\t\t`DELETE FROM allowed_users WHERE user_id = ?`, userID)\n\treturn err\n}\n\n// ListAllowedUsers returns all allowed users ordered by added_at.\nfunc (d *DB) ListAllowedUsers(ctx context.Context) ([]*AllowedUser, error) {\n\trows, err := d.db.QueryContext(ctx,\n\t\t`SELECT user_id, role, added_at FROM allowed_users ORDER BY added_at`)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn nil, err\n\t}\n\tdefer rows.Close()\n\n\tvar users []*AllowedUser\n\tfor rows.Next() {\n\t\tvar u AllowedUser\n\t\tvar addedAt string\n\t\tif err := rows.Scan(&u.UserID, &u.Role, &addedAt); err != nil {\n\t\t\treturn nil, err\n\t\t}\n\t\tu.AddedAt, _ = time.Parse(time.RFC3339, addedAt)\n\t\tusers = append(users, &u)\n\t}\n\treturn users, rows.Err()\n}\n\n// EnsureAdminUser ensures a user exists as an admin in the database.\n// If the user doesn't exist, they are added with the admin role.\n// If they exist with a different role, their role is updated to admin.\nfunc (d *DB) EnsureAdminUser(ctx context.Context, userID int64) error {\n\tu := &AllowedUser{\n\t\tUserID:  userID,\n\t\tRole:    \"admin\",\n\t\tAddedAt: time.Now().UTC(),\n\t}\n\t_, err := d.db.ExecContext(ctx,\n\t\t`INSERT INTO allowed_users (user_id, role, added_at) VALUES (?, ?, ?)\n\t\t ON CONFLICT(user_id) DO UPDATE SET role = excluded.role`,\n\t\tu.UserID, u.Role, u.AddedAt.UTC().Format(time.RFC3339),\n\t)\n\treturn err\n}\n\n// ── sent_messages ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n\n// RecordSentMessage stores a sent message so it can be found for editing later.\nfunc (d *DB) RecordSentMessage(ctx context.Context, m *SentMessage) error {\n\tif m.CreatedAt.IsZero() {\n\t\tm.CreatedAt = time.Now().UTC()\n\t}\n\t_, err := d.db.ExecContext(ctx,\n\t\t`INSERT OR IGNORE INTO sent_messages (chat_id, thread_id, The first chunk is sent as a reply\n154\t// to origMsgID; subsequent chunks are standalone messages in the same topic.\n155\t// Each sent message ID is stored in the sent_messages table.\n156\t//\n157\t// If a single code block exceeds 4096 characters, it is extracted and sent\n158\t// as a document attachment instead of being split mid-content.\n159\tfunc (s *Sender) SendResponse(ctx context.Context, chatID int64, threadID *int64, origMsgID int64, text string) error {\n160\t\t// First, extract any oversized code blocks and replace with placeholders\n161\t\tmodifiedText, oversizedBlocks := extractOversizedCodeBlocks(text)\n162\t\n163\t\t// Send the modified text (with placeholders for oversized blocks)\n164\t\tchunks := chunkText(modifiedText)\n165\t\tfor i, chunk := range chunks {\n166\t\t\treq := contract.SendRequest{\n167\t\t\t\tChatID:   chatID,\n168\t\t\t\tThreadID: threadID,\n169\t\t\t\tText:     chunk,\n170\t\t\t}\n171\t\t\tif i == 0 {\n172\t\t\t\treq.ReplyToMessageID = &origMsgID\n173\t\t\t}\n174\t\n175\t\t\tvar resp contract.SendResponse\n176\t\t\tif err := s.postWithRetry(ctx, \"/send\", req, &resp); err != nil {\n177\t\t\t\treturn fmt.Errorf(\"chunk %d: %w\", i, err)\n178\t\t\t}\n179\t\n180\t\t\tif _, dbErr := s.db.ExecContext(ctx,\n181\t\t\t\t`INSERT INTO sent_messages (chat_id, thread_id, orig_msg_id, sent_msg_id, chunk_index, created_at)\n182\t\t\t\t VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)`,\n183\t\t\t\tchatID, nullableInt64(threadID), origMsgID, resp.MessageID, i, time.Now().Unix(),\n184\t\t\t); dbErr != nil {\n185\t\t\t\tlog.Printf(\"[bridge/sender] db insert failed: %v\", dbErr)\n186\t\t\t}\n187\t\t}\n188\t\n189\t\t// Send oversized code blocks as document attachments\n190\t\tfor i, block := range oversizedBlocks {\n191\t\t\t// Only reply to the original message for the first document\n192\t\t\treplyTo := int64(0)\n193\t\t\tif i == 0 && len(chunks) == 0 {\n194\t\t\t\treplyTo = origMsgID\n195\t\t\t}\n196\t\n197\t\t\tcaption := fmt.Sprintf(\"Code block %d of %d (oversized)\", i+1, len(oversizedBlocks))\n198\t\t\tif err := s.SendDocument(ctx, chatID, threadID, replyTo, caption, block.filename, []byte(block.content)); err != nil {\n199\t\t\t\tlog.Printf(\"[bridge/sender] failed to send oversized code block %d as document: %v\", i+1, err)\n200\t\t\t\t// Continue with other blocks even if one fails\n201\t\t\t}\n202\t\t}\n203\t\n204\t\treturn nil\n205\t}\n206\t\n207\t// SendDocument sends a document via the proxy's /send_document endpoint.\n208\t// Used for oversized code blocks that exceed the 4096 character limit.\n209\tfunc (s *Sender) SendDocument(ctx context.Context, chatID int64, threadID *int64, origMsgID int64, caption string, filename string, content []byte) error {\n210\t\tvar body bytes.Buffer\n211\t\tw := multipart.NewWriter(&body)\n212\t\n213\t\t// Write metadata fields\n214\t\tif err := w.WriteField(\"chat_id\", fmt.Sprintf(\"%d\", chatID)); err != nil {\n215\t\t\treturn fmt.Errorf(\"write chat_id: %w\", err)\n216\t\t}\n217\t\tif threadID != nil {\n218\t\t\tif err := w.WriteField(\"thread_id\", fmt.Sprintf(\"%d\", *threadID)); err != nil {\n219\t\t\t\treturn fmt.Errorf(\"write thread_id: %w\", err)\n220\t\t\t}\n221\t\t}\n222\t\tif caption != \"\" {\n223\t\t\tif err := w.WriteField(\"caption\", caption); err != nil {\n224\t\t\t\treturn fmt.Errorf(\"write caption: %w\", err)\n225\t\t\t}\n226\t\t}\n227\t\tif origMsgID != 0 {\n228\t\t\tif err := w.WriteField(\"reply_to_message_id\", fmt.Sprintf(\"%d\", origMsgID)); err != nil {\n229\t\t\t\treturn fmt.Errorf(\"write reply_to_message_id: %w\", err)\n230\t\t\t}\n231\t\t}\n232\t\n233\t\t// Write the file content\n234\t\tfw, err := w.CreateFormFile(\"document\", filename)\n235\t\tif err != nil {\n236\t\t\treturn fmt.Errorf(\"create form file: %w\", err)\n237\t\t}\n238\t\tif _, err := fw.Write(content); err != nil {\n239\t\t\treturn fmt.Errorf(\"write file data: %w\", err)\n240\t\t}\n241\t\tif err := w.Close(); err != nil {\n242\t\t\treturn fmt.Errorf(\"close multipart writer: %w\", err)\n243\t\t}\n244\t\n245\t\treq, err := http.NewRequestWithContext(ctx, http.MethodPost, s.proxyURL+\"/send_document\", &body)\n246\t\tif err != nil {\n247\t\t\treturn fmt.Errorf(\"build request: %w\", err)\n248\t\t}\n249\t\treq.Header.Set(\"Content-Type\", w.FormDataContentType())\n250\t\n251\t\tresp, err := s.client.Do(req)\n252\t\tif err != nil {\n253\t\t\treturn fmt.Errorf(\"request: %w\", err)\n254\t\t}\n255\t\tdefer resp.Body.Close()\n256\t\n257\t\tif resp.StatusCode != http.StatusOK {\n258\t\t\tvar errResp contract.ErrorResponse\n259\t\t\t_ = json.NewDecoder(resp.Body).Decode(&errResp)\n260\t\t\tif errResp.ErrorCode == 0 {\n261\t\t\t\terrResp.ErrorCode = resp.StatusCode\n262\t\t\t}\n263\t\t\tif errResp.Description == \"\" {\n264\t\t\t\terrResp.Description = fmt.Sprintf(\"HTTP %d\", resp.StatusCode)\n265\t\t\t}\n266\t\t\treturn &errResp\n267\t\t}\n268\t\n269\t\treturn nil\n270\t}\n271\t\n272\t// SendToolApprovalPrompt Returns the new message ID for continued editing.\n415\tfunc (s *Sender) SendStreamOverflow(ctx context.Context, chatID int64, threadID *int64, text string) (int64, error) {\n416\t\treq := contract.SendRequest{\n417\t\t\tChatID:   chatID,\n418\t\t\tThreadID: threadID,\n419\t\t\tText:     text,\n420\t\t}\n421\t\tvar resp contract.SendResponse\n422\t\tif err := s.postWithRetry(ctx, \"/send\", req, &resp); err != nil {\n423\t\t\treturn 0, err\n424\t\t}\n425\t\t// Store in sent_messages table (chunk_index will be updated by caller)\n426\t\tif _, dbErr := s.db.ExecContext(ctx,\n427\t\t\t`INSERT INTO sent_messages (chat_id, thread_id, orig_msg_id, sent_msg_id, chunk_index, created_at)\n428\t\t\t VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)`,\n429\t\t\tchatID, nullableInt64(threadID), int64(0), resp.MessageID, 0, time.Now().Unix(),\n430\t\t); dbErr != nil {\n431\t\t\tlog.Printf(\"[bridge/sender] db insert failed: %v\", dbErr)\n432\t\t}\n433\t\treturn resp.MessageID, nil\n434\t}\n435\t\n436\t// SendStreamFinal concludes a streaming response. If streamMsgID is non-zero it\n437\t// edits that message with the first chunk of the final text and sends any\n438\t// overflow chunks as new (non-reply) messages in the same topic. If streamMsgID\n439\t// is zero and placeholderID is non-zero, it edits the placeholder with the result.\n440\t// If both are zero (no streaming occurred and no placeholder), it behaves exactly\n441\t// like SendResponse.\n442\tfunc (s *Sender) SendStreamFinal(ctx context.Context, chatID int64, threadID *int64, origMsgID, streamMsgID, placeholderID int64, text string) error {\n443\t\t// First, extract any oversized code blocks and replace with placeholders\n444\t\tmodifiedText, oversizedBlocks := extractOversizedCodeBlocks(text)\n445\t\n446\t\t// In summary mode: streamMsgID is 0, but placeholderID may be non-zero\n447\t\t// Edit the placeholder instead of sending a new message\n448\t\tif streamMsgID == 0 && placeholderID != 0 {\n449\t\t\tchunks := chunkText(modifiedText)\n450\t\t\tif err := s.postWithRetry(ctx, \"/edit\", contract.EditRequest{\n451\t\t\t\tChatID:    chatID,\n452\t\t\t\tMessageID: placeholderID,\n453\t\t\t\tText:      chunks[0],\n454\t\t\t}, nil); err != nil {\n455\t\t\t\tlog.Printf(\"[bridge/sender] placeholder edit failed: %v, falling back to new message\", err)\n456\t\t\t\treturn s.SendResponse(ctx, chatID, threadID, origMsgID, text)\n457\t\t\t}\n458\t\t\t// Send any overflow chunks as new messages in the topic\n459\t\t\tfor i, chunk := range chunks[1:] {\n460\t\t\t\treq := contract.SendRequest{\n461\t\t\t\t\tChatID:   chatID,\n462\t\t\t\t\tThreadID: threadID,\n463\t\t\t\t\tText:     chunk,\n464\t\t\t\t}\n465\t\t\t\tvar resp contract.SendResponse\n466\t\t\t\tif err := s.postWithRetry(ctx, \"/send\", req, &resp); err != nil {\n467\t\t\t\t\treturn fmt.Errorf(\"overflow chunk %d: %w\", i+1, err)\n468\t\t\t\t}\n469\t\t\t\tif _, dbErr := s.db.ExecContext(ctx,\n470\t\t\t\t\t`INSERT INTO sent_messages (chat_id, thread_id, orig_msg_id, sent_msg_id, chunk_index, created_at)\n471\t\t\t\t\t VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)`,\n472\t\t\t\t\tchatID, nullableInt64(threadID), origMsgID, resp.MessageID, i+1, time.Now().Unix(),\n473\t\t\t\t); dbErr != nil {\n474\t\t\t\t\tlog.Printf(\"[bridge/sender] db insert failed: %v\", dbErr)\n475\t\t\t\t}\n476\t\t\t}\n477\t\t\t// Send oversized code blocks as documents\n478\t\t\tfor _, block := range oversizedBlocks {\n479\t\t\t\tcaption := fmt.Sprintf(\"Oversized code block\")\n480\t\t\t\tif err := s.SendDocument(ctx, chatID, threadID, 0, caption, block.filename, []byte(block.content)); err != nil {\n481\t\t\t\t\tlog.Printf(\"[bridge/sender] failed to send oversized code block as document: %v\", err)\n482\t\t\t\t}\n483\t\t\t}\n484\t\t\treturn nil\n485\t\t}\n486\t\tif streamMsgID == 0 {\n487\t\t\treturn s.SendResponse(ctx, chatID, threadID, origMsgID, text)\n488\t\t}\n489\t\tchunks := chunkText(modifiedText)\n490\t\t// Edit the streaming placeholder with the first (or only) chunk.\n491\t\tif err := s.postWithRetry(ctx, \"/edit\", contract.EditRequest{\n492\t\t\tChatID:    chatID,\n493\t\t\tMessageID: streamMsgID,\n494\t\t\tText:      chunks[0],\n495\t\t}, nil); err != nil {\n496\t\t\tlog.Printf(\"[bridge/sender] stream final edit failed: %v, falling back to new message\", err)\n497\t\t\treturn s.SendResponse(ctx, chatID, threadID, origMsgID, text)\n498\t\t}\n499\t\t// Send any overflow chunks as new messages in the topic (no reply attribution).\n500\t\tfor i, chunk := range chunks[1:] {\n501\t\t\treq := contract.SendRequest{\n502\t\t\t\tChatID:   chatID,\n503\t\t\t\tThreadID: threadID,\n504\t\t\t\tText:     chunk,\n505\t\t\t}\n506\t\t\tvar resp contract.SendResponse\n507\t\t\tif err := s.postWithRetry(ctx, \"/send\", req, &resp); err != nil {\n508\t\t\t\treturn fmt.Errorf(\"overflow chunk %d: %w\", i+1, err)\n509\t\t\t}\n510\t\t\tif _, dbErr := s.db.ExecContext(ctx,\n511\t\t\t\t`INSERT INTO sent_messages (chat_id, thread_id, orig_msg_id, sent_msg_id, chunk_index, created_at)\n512\t\t\t\t VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)`,\n513\t\t\t\tchatID, nullableInt64(threadID), origMsgID, resp.MessageID, i+1, time.Now().Unix(),\n514\t\t\t); dbErr != nil {\n515\t\t\t\tlog.Printf(\"[bridge/sender] db insert failed: %v\", dbErr)\n516\t\t\t}\n517\t\t}\n518\t\t// Send oversized code blocks as documents\n519\t\tfor _, block := range oversizedBlocks {\n520\t\t\tcaption := fmt.Sprintf(\"Oversized code block\")\n521\t\t\tif err := s.SendDocument(ctx, chatID, threadID, 0, caption, block.filename, []byte(block.content)); err != nil {\n522\t\t\t\tlog.Printf(\"[bridge/sender] failed to send oversized code block as document: %v\", err)\n523\t\t\t}\n524\t\t}\n525\t\treturn nil\n526\t}\n527\t\n528\t// chunkText splits HTML text into ≤4096-rune chunks at natural boundaries.\n529\t//\n530\t// Algorithm:\n531\t//  1. If text ≤ 4096 runes → return as-is.\n532\t//  2. Identify code block boundaries (<pre>...</pre>).\n533\t//  3. Find split points in priority order:\n534\t//     a) Paragraph break (\\n\\n) outside code blocks\n535\t//     b) After </pre> (between code blocks)\n536\t//     c) Single newline (\\n) outside code blocks\n537\t//     d) Hard cut at 4096 (last resort)\n538\t//  4. When splitting, close unclosed HTML tags at split point and reopen in next chunk.\n539\tfunc chunkText(text string) []string {\n540\t\tif runeLen(text) <= maxMessageLen {\n541\t\t\treturn []string{text}\n542\t\t}\n543\t\n544\t\t// Find all code block boundaries to know where we can't split.\n545\t\tblocks := findCodeBlocks(text)\n546\t\n547\t\tvar chunks []string\n548\t\topenTags := \"\" // Tags that need reopening in next chunk (e.g., \"<b><pre>\")\n549\t\tremaining := text\n550\t\toffset := 0    // Byte offset into the original text\n551\t\n552\t\tfor len(remaining) > 0 {\n553\t\t\tif runeLen(remaining) <= maxMessageLen {\n554\t\t\t\t// Final chunk — prepend any open tags, then close them\n555\t\t\t\tchunk := openTags + remaining\n556\t\t\t\tbalanced, _ := balanceTags(chunk, \"\")\n557\t\t\t\tchunks = append(chunks, balanced)\n558\t\t\t\tbreak\n559\t\t\t}\n560\t\n561\t\t\t// Find split point based on remaining content (not including open code block exceeds 4096 characters, it is extracted and sent\n// as a document attachment instead of being split mid-content.\nfunc (s *Sender) SendResponse(ctx context.Context, chatID int64, threadID *int64, origMsgID int64, text string) error {\n\t// First, extract any oversized code blocks and replace with placeholders\n\tmodifiedText, oversizedBlocks := extractOversizedCodeBlocks(text)\n\n\t// Send the modified text (with placeholders for oversized blocks)\n\tchunks := chunkText(modifiedText)\n\tfor i, chunk := range chunks {\n\t\treq := contract.SendRequest{\n\t\t\tChatID:   chatID,\n\t\t\tThreadID: threadID,\n\t\t\tText:     chunk,\n\t\t}\n\t\tif i == 0 {\n\t\t\treq.ReplyToMessageID = &origMsgID\n\t\t}\n\n\t\tvar resp contract.SendResponse\n\t\tif err := s.postWithRetry(ctx, \"/send\", req, &resp); err != nil {\n\t\t\treturn fmt.Errorf(\"chunk %d: %w\", i, err)\n\t\t}\n\n\t\tif _, dbErr := s.db.ExecContext(ctx,\n\t\t\t`INSERT INTO sent_messages (chat_id, thread_id, orig_msg_id, sent_msg_id, chunk_index, created_at)\n\t\t\t VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)`,\n\t\t\tchatID, nullableInt64(threadID), origMsgID, resp.MessageID, i, time.Now().Unix(),\n\t\t); dbErr != nil {\n\t\t\tlog.Printf(\"[bridge/sender] db insert failed: %v\", dbErr)\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\n\t// Send oversized code blocks as document attachments\n\tfor i, block := range oversizedBlocks {\n\t\t// Only reply to the original message for the first document\n\t\treplyTo := int64(0)\n\t\tif i == 0 && len(chunks) == 0 {\n\t\t\treplyTo = origMsgID\n\t\t}\n\n\t\tcaption := fmt.Sprintf(\"Code block %d of %d (oversized)\", i+1, len(oversizedBlocks))\n\t\tif err := s.SendDocument(ctx, chatID, threadID, replyTo, caption, block.filename, []byte(block.content)); err != nil {\n\t\t\tlog.Printf(\"[bridge/sender] failed to send oversized code block %d as document: %v\", i+1, err)\n\t\t\t// Continue with other blocks even if one fails\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\n\treturn nil\n}\n\n// SendDocument sends a document via the proxy's /send_document endpoint.\n// Used for oversized code blocks that exceed the 4096 character limit.\nfunc (s *Sender) SendDocument(ctx context.Context, chatID int64, threadID *int64, origMsgID int64, caption string, filename string, content []byte) error {\n\tvar body bytes.Buffer\n\tw := multipart.NewWriter(&body)\n\n\t// Write metadata fields\n\tif err := w.WriteField(\"chat_id\", fmt.Sprintf(\"%d\", chatID)); err != nil {\n\t\treturn fmt.Errorf(\"write chat_id: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\tif threadID != nil {\n\t\tif err := w.WriteField(\"thread_id\", fmt.Sprintf(\"%d\", *threadID)); err != nil {\n\t\t\treturn fmt.Errorf(\"write thread_id: %w\", err)\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\tif caption != \"\" {\n\t\tif err := w.WriteField(\"caption\", caption); err != nil {\n\t\t\treturn fmt.Errorf(\"write caption: %w\", err)\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\tif origMsgID != 0 {\n\t\tif err := w.WriteField(\"reply_to_message_id\", fmt.Sprintf(\"%d\", origMsgID)); err != nil {\n\t\t\treturn fmt.Errorf(\"write reply_to_message_id: %w\", err)\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\n\t// Write the file content\n\tfw, err := w.CreateFormFile(\"document\", filename)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn fmt.Errorf(\"create form file: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\tif _, err := fw.Write(content); err != nil {\n\t\treturn fmt.Errorf(\"write file data: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\tif err := w.Close(); err != nil {\n\t\treturn fmt.Errorf(\"close multipart writer: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\n\treq, err := http.NewRequestWithContext(ctx, http.MethodPost, s.proxyURL+\"/send_document\", &body)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn fmt.Errorf(\"build request: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\treq.Header.Set(\"Content-Type\", w.FormDataContentType())\n\n\tresp, err := s.client.Do(req)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn fmt.Errorf(\"request: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\tdefer resp.Body.Close()\n\n\tif resp.StatusCode != http.StatusOK {\n\t\tvar errResp contract.ErrorResponse\n\t\t_ = json.NewDecoder(resp.Body).Decode(&errResp)\n\t\tif errResp.ErrorCode == 0 {\n\t\t\terrResp.ErrorCode = resp.StatusCode\n\t\t}\n\t\tif errResp.Description == \"\" {\n\t\t\terrResp.Description = fmt.Sprintf(\"HTTP %d\", resp.StatusCode)\n\t\t}\n\t\treturn &errResp\n\t}\n\n\treturn nil\n}\n\n// SendToolApprovalPrompt sends a tool approval prompt with approve/deny inline keyboard buttons.\n// *ptr\n}\n\n// sendInitialStream sends the first streaming message as a reply to origMsgID\n// and returns the sent message ID. Used by invokeClaudeAPI to post the initial\n// live-edit placeholder.\nfunc (s *Sender) sendInitialStream(ctx context.Context, chatID int64, threadID *int64, origMsgID int64, text string) (int64, error) {\n\treq := contract.SendRequest{\n\t\tChatID:           chatID,\n\t\tThreadID:         threadID,\n\t\tText:             text,\n\t\tReplyToMessageID: &origMsgID,\n\t}\n\tvar resp contract.SendResponse\n\tif err := s.postWithRetry(ctx, \"/send\", req, &resp); Returns the new message ID for continued editing.\nfunc (s *Sender) SendStreamOverflow(ctx context.Context, chatID int64, threadID *int64, text string) (int64, error) {\n\treq := contract.SendRequest{\n\t\tChatID:   chatID,\n\t\tThreadID: threadID,\n\t\tText:     text,\n\t}\n\tvar resp contract.SendResponse\n\tif err := s.postWithRetry(ctx, \"/send\", req, &resp); err != nil {\n\t\treturn 0, err\n\t}\n\t// Store in sent_messages table (chunk_index will be updated by caller)\n\tif _, dbErr := s.db.ExecContext(ctx,\n\t\t`INSERT INTO sent_messages (chat_id, thread_id, orig_msg_id, sent_msg_id, chunk_index, created_at)\n\t\t VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)`,\n\t\tchatID, nullableInt64(threadID), int64(0), resp.MessageID, 0, time.Now().Unix(),\n\t); dbErr != nil {\n\t\tlog.Printf(\"[bridge/sender] db insert failed: %v\", dbErr)\n\t}\n\treturn resp.MessageID, nil\n}\n\n// SendStreamFinal concludes a streaming response. If streamMsgID is non-zero it\n// edits that message with the first chunk of the final text and sends any\n// overflow chunks as new (non-reply) messages in the same topic. If streamMsgID\n// is zero and placeholderID is non-zero, it edits the placeholder with the result.\n// If both are zero (no streaming occurred and no placeholder), it behaves exactly\n// like SendResponse.\nfunc (s *Sender) SendStreamFinal(ctx context.Context, chatID int64, threadID *int64, origMsgID, streamMsgID, placeholderID int64, text string) error {\n\t// First, extract any oversized code blocks and replace with placeholders\n\tmodifiedText, oversizedBlocks := extractOversizedCodeBlocks(text)\n\n\t// In summary mode: streamMsgID is 0, but placeholderID may be non-zero\n\t// Edit the placeholder instead of sending a new message\n\tif streamMsgID == 0 && placeholderID != 0 {\n\t\tchunks := chunkText(modifiedText)\n\t\tif err := s.postWithRetry(ctx, \"/edit\", contract.EditRequest{\n\t\t\tChatID:    chatID,\n\t\t\tMessageID: placeholderID,\n\t\t\tText:      chunks[0],\n\t\t}, nil); err != nil {\n\t\t\tlog.Printf(\"[bridge/sender] placeholder edit failed: %v, falling back to new message\", err)\n\t\t\treturn s.SendResponse(ctx, chatID, threadID, origMsgID, text)\n\t\t}\n\t\t// Send any overflow chunks as new messages in the topic\n\t\tfor i, chunk := range chunks[1:] {\n\t\t\treq := contract.SendRequest{\n\t\t\t\tChatID:   chatID,\n\t\t\t\tThreadID: threadID,\n\t\t\t\tText:     chunk,\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\tvar resp contract.SendResponse\n\t\t\tif err := s.postWithRetry(ctx, \"/send\", req, &resp); err != nil {\n\t\t\t\treturn fmt.Errorf(\"overflow chunk %d: %w\", i+1, err)\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\tif _, dbErr := s.db.ExecContext(ctx,\n\t\t\t\t`INSERT INTO sent_messages (chat_id, thread_id, orig_msg_id, sent_msg_id, chunk_index, created_at)\n\t\t\t\t VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)`,\n\t\t\t\tchatID, nullableInt64(threadID), origMsgID, resp.MessageID, i+1, time.Now().Unix(),\n\t\t\t); dbErr != nil {\n\t\t\t\tlog.Printf(\"[bridge/sender] db insert failed: %v\", dbErr)\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t}\n\t\t// Send oversized code blocks as documents\n\t\tfor _, block := range oversizedBlocks {\n\t\t\tcaption := fmt.Sprintf(\"Oversized code block\")\n\t\t\tif err := s.SendDocument(ctx, chatID, threadID, 0, caption, block.filename, []byte(block.content)); err != nil {\n\t\t\t\tlog.Printf(\"[bridge/sender] failed to send oversized code block as document: %v\", err)\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t}\n\t\treturn nil\n\t}\n\tif streamMsgID == 0 {\n\t\treturn s.SendResponse(ctx, chatID, threadID, origMsgID, text)\n\t}\n\tchunks := chunkText(modifiedText)\n\t// Edit the streaming placeholder with the first (or only) chunk.\n\tif err := s.postWithRetry(ctx, \"/edit\", contract.EditRequest{\n\t\tChatID:    chatID,\n\t\tMessageID: streamMsgID,\n\t\tText:      chunks[0],\n\t}, nil); err != nil {\n\t\tlog.Printf(\"[bridge/sender] stream final edit failed: %v, falling back to new message\", err)\n\t\treturn s.SendResponse(ctx, chatID, threadID, origMsgID, text)\n\t}\n\t// Send any overflow chunks as new messages in the topic (no reply attribution).\n\tfor i, chunk := range chunks[1:] {\n\t\treq := contract.SendRequest{\n\t\t\tChatID:   chatID,\n\t\t\tThreadID: threadID,\n\t\t\tText:     chunk,\n\t\t}\n\t\tvar resp contract.SendResponse\n\t\tif err := s.postWithRetry(ctx, \"/send\", req, &resp); err != nil {\n\t\t\treturn fmt.Errorf(\"overflow chunk %d: %w\", i+1, err)\n\t\t}\n\t\tif _, dbErr := s.db.ExecContext(ctx,\n\t\t\t`INSERT INTO sent_messages (chat_id, thread_id, orig_msg_id, sent_msg_id, chunk_index, created_at)\n\t\t\t VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)`,\n\t\t\tchatID, nullableInt64(threadID), origMsgID, resp.MessageID, i+1, time.Now().Unix(),\n\t\t); dbErr != nil {\n\t\t\tlog.Printf(\"[bridge/sender] db insert failed: %v\", dbErr)\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\t// Send oversized code blocks as documents\n\tfor _, block := range oversizedBlocks {\n\t\tcaption := fmt.Sprintf(\"Oversized code block\")\n\t\tif err := s.SendDocument(ctx, chatID, threadID, 0, caption, block.filename, []byte(block.content)); err != nil {\n\t\t\tlog.Printf(\"[bridge/sender] failed to send oversized code block as document: %v\", err)\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\treturn nil\n}\n\n// chunkText splits HTML text into ≤4096-rune chunks at natural boundaries.\n//\n// Algorithm:\n//  1. If text ≤ 4096 runes → return as-is.\n//  2. Identify code block boundaries (<pre>...</pre>).\n//  3. Find split points in priority order:\n//     a) Paragraph break (\\n\\n) outside code blocks\n//     b) After </pre> (between code blocks)\n//     c) Single newline (\\n) outside code blocks\n//     d) Hard cut at 4096 (last resort)\n//  4. When splitting, close unclosed HTML tags at split point and reopen in next chunk.\nfunc chunkText(text string) []string {\n\tif runeLen(text) <= maxMessageLen {\n\t\treturn []string{text}\n\t}\n\n\t// Find all code block boundaries to know where we can't split.\n\tblocks := findCodeBlocks(text)\n\n\tvar chunks []string\n\topenTags := \"\" // Tags that need reopening in next chunk (e.g., \"<b><pre>\")\n\tremaining := text\n\toffset := 0    // Byte offset into the original text\n\n\tfor len(remaining) > 0 {\n\t\tif runeLen(remaining) <= maxMessageLen {\n\t\t\t// Final chunk — prepend any open tags, then close them\n\t\t\tchunk := openTags + remaining\n\t\t\tbalanced, _ := balanceTags(chunk, \"\")\n\t\t\tchunks = append(chunks, balanced)\n\t\t\tbreak\n\t\t}\n\n\t\t// Find split point based on remaining content (not including open tags)\n\t\twindowBytes := runeByteLen(remaining, maxMessageLen)\n\t\tsplitIdx := findBestSplitPoint(remaining[:windowBytes], blocks, offset)\n\n\t\tif splitIdx <= 0 {\n\t\t\tsplitIdx = windowBytes\n\t\t}\n\n\t\t// Balance tags in this chunk (prepend open tags from previous split)\n\t\tchunkContent := openTags + remaining[:splitIdx]\n\t\tchunk, newOpenTags := balanceTags(chunkContent, \"\")\n\t\tchunks = append(chunks, chunk)\n\n\t\t// Set up for next iteration\n\t\toffset += splitIdx\n\t\tremaining = remaining[splitIdx:]\n\t\t// newOpenTags.reopenTags are the tags we need to reopen\n\t\topenTags = newOpenTags.reopenTags\n\t}\n\n\treturn chunks\n}\n\n// codeBlock represents a <pre>...</pre> region in the HTML.\ntype codeBlock struct {\n\tstart int // byte offset of <\n\tend   int // byte offset after >\n}\n\n// findCodeBlocks scans HTML for all <pre>...</pre> blocks and returns their positions.\nfunc findCodeBlocks(html string) []codeBlock {\n\tvar blocks []codeBlock\n\ti := 0\n\tfor {\n\t\tstart := strings.Index(html[i:], \"<pre>\")\n\t\tif start < 0 {\n\t\t\tbreak\n\t\t}\n\t\tstart += i\n\t\tend := strings.Index(html[start:], \"</pre>\")\n\t\tif end < 0 {\n\t\t\t// Unclosed block — treat rest as code block\n\t\t\tblocks = append(blocks, codeBlock{start: start, end: len(html)})\n\t\t\tbreak\n\t\t}\n\t\tend += start + 6 // Position after </pre>\n\t\tblocks = append(blocks, codeBlock{start: start, end: end})\n\t\ti = end\n\t}\n\treturn blocks\n}\n\n// isInsideCodeBlock returns true if position is within any code block.\nfunc isInsideCodeBlock(pos int, blocks []codeBlock) bool {\n\tfor _, b := range blocks {\n\t\tif pos >= b.start && pos < b.end {\n\t\t\treturn true\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\treturn false\n}\n\n// findBestSplitPoint finds the optimal position to split text within window,\n// respecting code block boundaries. offset is the byte offset of window within\n// the original text (for code block position calculations).\nfunc findBestSplitPoint(window string, blocks []codeBlock, offset int) int {\n\t// Priority 1a: Paragraph break (\\n\\n) outside code blocks\n\tlastIdx := 0\n\tfor {\n\t\tidx := strings.Index(window[lastIdx:], \"\\n\\n\")\n\t\tif idx < 0 {\n\t\t\tbreak\n\t\t}\n\t\tpos := lastIdx + idx\n\t\t// Convert relative position to absolute for code block check\n\t\tabsPos := offset + pos\n\t\tif !isInsideCodeBlock(absPos, blocks) {\n\t\t\treturn pos + 2 // Split after the \\n\\n\n\t\t}\n\t\tlastIdx = pos + 2\n\t}\n\n\t// Priority 1b: After </pre> (between code blocks)\n\tfor _, b := range blocks {\n\t\t// Convert absolute block end to relative position in window\n\t\trelEnd := b.end - offset\n\t\tif relEnd > 0 && relEnd < len(window) {\n\t\t\t// Make sure we're actually at a split boundary\n\t\t\tif strings.HasPrefix(window[relEnd:], \"\\n\") {\n\t\t\t\treturn relEnd + 1\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\treturn relEnd\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\n\t// Priority 1c: Single newline (\\n) outside code blocks\n\tlastIdx = 0\n\tfor {\n\t\tidx := strings.Index(window[lastIdx:], \"\\n\")\n\t\tif idx < 0 {\n\t\t\tbreak\n\t\t}\n\t\tpos := lastIdx + idx\n\t\t// Convert relative position to absolute for code block check\n\t\tabsPos := offset + pos\n\t\tif !isInsideCodeBlock(absPos, blocks) {\n\t\t\treturn pos + 1\n\t\t}\n\t\tlastIdx = pos + 1\n\t}\n\n\t// Priority 1d: No good split found — caller will hard cut\n\treturn -1\n}\n\n// tagBalanceResult contains tags to close at end of chunk and tags to reopen at start of next.\ntype tagBalanceResult struct {\n\tcloseTags  string // e.g., \"</b></pre>\"\n\treopenTags string // e.g., \"<b><pre>\"\n}\n\n// balanceTags ensures HTML tags are balanced at the split point.\n// Returns the chunk with close tags appended, and the tags that need reopening.\nfunc balanceTags(chunk string, currentOpen string) (string, tagBalanceResult) {\n\t// Track which tags are open in this chunk\n\tvar openStack []string\n\ti := 0\n\n\tfor i < len(chunk) {\n\t\t// Find next tag start\n\t\tif chunk[i] != '<' {\n\t\t\ti++\n\t\t\tcontinue\n\t\t}\n\n\t\t// Find tag end\n\t\ttagEnd := strings.IndexByte(chunk[i:], '>')\n\t\tif tagEnd < 0 {\n\t\t\tbreak\n\t\t}\n\t\ttagEnd += i\n\n\t\ttag := chunk[i+1 : tagEnd]\n\t\tisClosing := strings.HasPrefix(tag,
